# OpenAI mathematics manuscripts

This appendix indexes all **719 active manuscripts** in the [official manuscript map](https://github.com/openai/math/blob/1581d4cbdd69a0faf34b2089614fe2a814316a6a/CONTENTS.md), pinned to commit `1581d4cbdd69a0faf34b2089614fe2a814316a6a`. Every listed PDF path was checked against the complete `preprints/` Git tree at that commit.

The date below is the **6 October 2026 public collection announcement**, not a date inferred from an internal manuscript filename. These works were produced by an internal OpenAI model. Their verification status varies; listing a manuscript does not certify its mathematical claims. The source repository supplies revisions, citations, and available Lean proofs.

These manuscript entries are separate from the main AI-paper count. Some mathematical results may also appear in earlier announcement papers, so counts across the two lists should not be added as a count of distinct results.

The source history also records three withdrawals: *Algebraicity of Weil classes on split abelian eightfolds*, *Algebraicity of Kuga–Satake Correspondences for K3 Surfaces*, and *The rational Hodge conjecture for products of K3 surfaces*. They are excluded from the active list below; see the [withdrawal notices](https://github.com/openai/math/blob/1581d4cbdd69a0faf34b2089614fe2a814316a6a/history.md).

From scale symmetry to local conformal symmetry in four-dimensional QFT.** Under the stated bounded-local-net and field-reconstruction hypotheses, proves that scale symmetry implies local conformal symmetry for four-dimensional unitary positive-energy theories with a discrete bounded-below scaling spectrum of finite multiplicity, finite scaling support and a physical local scale current. The stress tensor has a traceless improvement with unchanged spacetime charges. The conclusion concerns local Ward identities, not a global conformal action on the whole net.  </td> </tr></tbody> <tbody><tr> <td>  &emsp;[Scale and conformal symmetry in four-dimensional operational quantum field theory | [Source](https://github.com/openai/math/blob/1581d4cbdd69a0faf34b2089614fe2a814316a6a/CONTENTS.md) · [Paper](https://github.com/openai/math/blob/1581d4cbdd69a0faf34b2089614fe2a814316a6a/preprints/Scale-and-conformal-symmetry-in-four-dimensional-operational-quantum-field-theory-September-26-2026/main.pdf) |
